TDD protocol:

1. test-engineer writes the next failing test (RED) — or a GREEN-ONLY pass-through for cases that state a build-only check.
2. software-engineer makes the test green (RED flow) or implements the story's `## Change` for the forwarded cases (GREEN-ONLY flow).
3. test-engineer confirms GREEN (or runs a build-only check for GREEN-ONLY), then either opens the next case or runs the full Verification Gate and appends IMPLEMENTATION_READY_FOR_REVIEW.
4. A story declaring "Executor: groundwork-engineer" is not the test-engineer's to open. Its cases are the groundwork executor's, and they are green when a GROUNDWORK-COMPLETE line names that story.

## TEST-ENGINEER — R3 · the promised expiry, the lost objective run and the explicit recovery

**Cycle.** RED for the routed change `R3` (`.agents/plan/epics/050.2-the-run-renew-release-and-report.md`, `docs/proposal/phase-2/runs-and-exclusion.md` "A promised expiry is kept, and no recovery is silent", `docs/proposal/api/execution.md` "The objective scope of a claim" and "node.renew").

**Test written.**

- file: `src/commands/run/renew-run.test.ts` (edited) — suite: `src/commands/run/renew-run.test` — new methods: `a renew announces the objective run's expiry as objectiveExpiresAt`, `an objective renew reports its own expiry as objectiveExpiresAt`, `a task renew leaves an objective run at its own max_lifetime_at and still renews the task run`, `the expiry pass after a lifetime-bound renew ends the objective run once and mints no replacement`, `a task renew whose objective holds no active run is refused objective-run-lost and writes nothing`, `a task renew whose objective run the expiry pass just ended is refused objective-run-lost and rolls the expiry back`, `an ended objective run is refused objective-run-lost, not run-ended`
- asserts: a renew never moves a run past its own `max_lifetime_at`, it announces the objective run's deadline, and it refuses rather than continue over an objective that holds no active run.
- fixture change in the same file: `seedFixture` now seeds the structural objective run by default for a task run, because after `objective-run-lost` a task renew without one is a refusal. The three tests that seeded it themselves stop doing so. No shipped assertion changed; the file was green before and after that edit alone.
- file: `src/commands/node/claim-node.test.ts` (edited) — suite: `src/commands/node/claim-node.test` — new methods: `a claim recovers objective authority after the structural run reached its lifetime`, `a claim on a running objective is refused subtree-busy while a task run under it is live`, `a claim on a running objective writes no node state change`, `a claim on an objective that is neither ready nor running is refused illegal-transition`
- asserts: end to end — a task claim, the objective run reaching its lifetime and being ended by the pass, a task report that still succeeds under its own authority, an attest on the old objective run refused `run-ended`, then a claim on the `running` objective that opens a fresh structural run at fence 1 whose authority a following attest accepts.
- new local helpers in that file: `reportTaskAccepted`, `attestObjective`, `refusedAttest`.
- file: `src/http/contract/errors.test.ts` (edited) — the three pinned positional arrays gain `objective-run-lost` after `lifetime-exceeded`.
- file: `src/http/contract/example.test.ts` (edited) — new methods: `nodeRenewResponse carries objectiveExpiresAt and its example parses`, `the node.renew objectiveExpiresAt field decision is published`.

**Controls.** `a claim on a running objective is refused subtree-busy while a task run under it is live` and `a claim on an objective that is neither ready nor running is refused illegal-transition` pass now and must stay green: they prove the recovery cannot take authority from live work and cannot widen the state gate beyond `running`. The control for `objective-run-lost` is the second half of its own test — the same fixture with a live objective run renews both runs. `the expiry pass after a lifetime-bound renew ends the objective run once and mints no replacement` is a characterization test: it pins shipped expiry behaviour and passes now; its sensitivity to the clamp is carried by `a task renew leaves an objective run at its own max_lifetime_at and still renews the task run`, which is red.

**RED proof.**

- command: `pnpm test -- src/commands/run/renew-run.test.ts src/commands/node/claim-node.test.ts src/http/contract/example.test.ts src/http/contract/errors.test.ts`
